## Current runtime

- New scheduling_inputs.py owns same sorted/schema-quoted input table list and OT calendar when enabled. lock_inputs holds existing PostgreSQL SHARE ROW EXCLUSIVE locks, then expires only clean non-PK mapped attributes/relationships on loaded scheduling rows. Dirty caller columns/collections and pending new rows preserved; helper itself never flushes/commits. Flag-off behavior unchanged. rota_compute.lock_operational_inputs delegates to it so existing entry points gain clean-state freshness.

Rechecks draft/locked/active-rule authority BEFORE deleting/creating slots. No stale pre-lock rule DTO retained. No initializer allowed on this under-lock read.
- finish_bootstrap helper used for default call clusters, rule version and rule setting creation. If root transaction has acquired scheduling input locks, bootstrap flushes instead of commits; otherwise prior normal bootstrap commit behavior stays. db.info scheduling_input_transaction marker is COMMIT OWNERSHIP ONLY, NEVER proof a lock remains held after nested rollback. It is matched to active current root so completed/rolled-back transaction cannot poison later behavior. SQLite tests prove rollback removes defaults and normal later bootstrap still commits; PG cold cases prove writers remain actually blocked.
- Found legacy rota_auto_fill cached route lacked operational lock (context skips standalone assignment lock). Now acquire final lock after deliberate audit-run commit, freshly select period/open slots, check draft and only then build context. Both cached and uncached paths preserve OT. No new permission/scope requirement beyond existing draft restriction. Final period lookup is explicit SELECT, not potentially stale identity get. Failure preserves prior audit failed-run semantics.

# Latest checkpoint - template OT unit/shuffle integration verified, 14 November 2026

Supersedes all older checkpoints. Full50goal active/approved:8Done(N01,N03,N07-N12),4In progress(N02/N04/N13/N14),38Queued. ## Current source and verified behavior

- New ot_template.TemplateOtCapacity is a private read-only capacity checker. Loads candidate_context once with prospective slots, fixed duties/units, strict ExtraDutyContext. Checks complete proposed unit-assigned/unfilled layouts, using canonical strict call/group/posting/leave/rest primitives, existing optimizer.incompatible and joint OT reservation graph. CP chooses one private person per planned slot; independent physical and OT checks verify witness. Person hints are temporary/in-memory only; no assignments saved/serialized. This is OT/strict-duty capacity, NOT person monthly-limit/preference/fairness or global unit-optimality certification.
- Slot copies get stable unique IDs when ORM slots are pending so independent checks cannot accidentally treat all id=None rows as one slot. Cache by full immutable slot signatures; only feasible/proven-infeasible cached, unknown not cached. Limits512slots/200000vars before further creation/2M pair work/64 validation attempts; candidate cache2048/result128; private eligibility cache bounded. Uses dated unit_contexts prefilter, canonical eligibility retained. No SQL after snapshot load (tested).
- candidate_context now accepts prospective_slots keyword, passing it to monthly guard once; guard input range and nearby preserved assignments therefore include real template end/rest.
- rota_template generation uses OT check callback before original fairness comparison and forced fallback. OT-infeasible/unknown units excluded; if every applicable unit lacks proof, create explicit null-unit UNRESOLVED DutySlot with per-unit blocked audit and incomplete summary (not forced exception). Reviewed-zero demand retains legacy non-OT forced review. Hypothetical people never committed. Existing operational-input lock acquired when flag enabled; see unresolved freshness caveat below.
- Vertical shuffle invokes full-layout capacity check on beneficial proposals, restores temporary unit mutations in finally, rejects unknown/infeasible swaps. Existing pattern/fairness order unchanged. Final summary ot_capacity names unit-assigned capacity scope and unallocated count. Existing null-unit rendering inspected; workbook export tested; browser-wide acceptance not yet verified.
